### [139] [Energy Efficiency in Microservice Architectures: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04070)
*Eoan O'Dea,Roberta Capuano,Paris Avgeriou,Henry Muccini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述（遵循Kitchenham指南）对微服务架构中的能源效率研究进行了综合梳理，分析了40项主要研究，发现能源效率主要被视为运行时操作优化问题（如监控、调度、资源管理），而设计阶段的集成有限，测量实践主要依赖基础设施层面的模型估算和粗粒度监控，亟需更早的架构级整合和改进的测量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可持续软件研究日益增长，但微服务能源效率的研究分散在运营、基础设施和架构等不同视角，彼此孤立。现有研究缺乏综合性的综述来系统梳理能源效率在架构层面如何被考虑、测量和解决，这一空白阻碍了该领域的整体进展。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，遵循Kitchenham的指南，从四个主要数字图书馆筛选文献，通过六阶段流程并辅以后向和前向滚雪球式检索，最终筛选出40项主要研究进行分析。

Result: 研究发现能源效率主要在运行时通过监控、调度和资源管理等方式被关注，而设计阶段的集成仍然有限；测量实践主要以基础设施为导向，依赖模型估算和粗粒度监控手段。

Conclusion: 微服务中的能源效率目前主要被当作运营优化问题而非覆盖整个生命周期的架构关注点，这凸显了在架构设计早期阶段整合能源效率考量以及改进测量实践的必要性。

Abstract: Context. Microservice architectures are widely adopted for building scalable cloud-native systems, enabling independent deployment, fine-grained service composition, and operational elasticity. Problem. Despite growing interest in sustainable software, research on energy efficiency in microservices spans operational, infrastructural, and architectural perspectives, but these are typically addressed in isolation. Existing studies focus on optimisation techniques or measurement approaches, with limited synthesis of how energy efficiency is considered, measured, and addressed at the architectural level. Goal. This study synthesises research on energy-efficient microservices by examining where energy efficiency is considered, how it is measured, and which architectural solutions have been proposed. Method. We conduct a systematic literature review following Kitchenham's guidelines, screening publications from four major digital libraries through a six-stage process with backward and forward snowballing, resulting in 40 primary studies. Results. Energy efficiency is predominantly addressed at runtime through monitoring, scheduling, and resource management, while design-time integration remains limited. Measurement practices are largely infrastructure-oriented and rely on model-based estimation and coarse-grained monitoring. Conclusion. Energy efficiency in microservices is primarily treated as an operational optimisation problem rather than a lifecycle-spanning architectural concern, highlighting the need for earlier architectural integration and improved measurement practices.

</details>


### [140] [An Approach for Embedding-Guided Function Reuse Detection in Embedded C Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04137)
*A A Talha Talukder,Omar Alam,Akramul Azim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向嵌入式C软件复用检测的领域感知RAG管道，通过结合注释、调用图和README的语义丰富，以及四种硬件兼容性验证器，解决了传统静态分析工具无法检测硬件域兼容性的问题。在6个嵌入式项目（184个函数，4815对候选）上，管道将SonarQube作为复用过滤器时的93.6%误报率显著降低，硬件兼容性验证器准确率达97.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式软件函数在不同微控制器平台间的复用在经济上很有价值，但技术实现困难：即使函数间余弦相似度超过0.90且通过SonarQube质量检查，在不同硬件平台上也可能完全不兼容。现有静态分析工具（如SonarQube）的设计目标是衡量代码质量而非硬件域兼容性，缺乏对外设接口、硬件抽象层（HAL）依赖和寄存器映射约束的建模能力，因此无法有效支持嵌入式代码复用检测。

Method: 本文构建了一个领域感知的检索增强生成（RAG）管道。管道首先通过提取函数的行内注释、调用图上下文和项目README对每个函数进行语义增强，然后使用八种骨干模型（MiniLM、MPNet、BGE、E5、GraphCodeBERT、OpenAI text-embedding-3-small、LLaMA 3 8B、StarCoder2 3B）作为特征提取器进行嵌入。随后，四种硬件兼容性验证器——外设令牌重叠、参数数量一致性、调用图依赖重叠和结构化分支模式——直接在检索堆栈中对候选函数进行过滤。

Result: 在6个公开嵌入式C软件项目（184个函数，4815对候选）上的评估显示：SonarQube作为复用过滤器产生93.6%的误报率，其中83.5%的失败由静态分析无法检测的硬件环境不匹配导致。对40对拒绝候选的人工验证确认验证器准确率达97.5%。诊断性规则注入变体识别出主要失败类别（McNemar卡方≈294.0，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 该领域感知RAG管道能够直接在检索堆栈中嵌入硬件兼容性验证，有效弥补了静态分析与嵌入式硬件域兼容性之间的鸿沟，显著降低了代码复用检测中的误报率，并为嵌入式软件复用提供了更可靠的检索方案。

Abstract: Reusing embedded software functions across products is economically valuable but technically difficult: the same functionality implemented for two different microcontroller platforms can be entirely incompatible at the hardware level, even when the functions score above 0.90 cosine similarity and both pass SonarQube quality checks. Static analysis tools were designed to measure code quality, not hardware-domain compatibility, and have no model of peripheral interfaces, hardware abstraction layer (HAL) dependencies, or register-map constraints. This paper presents a domain-aware retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline for embedded C software reuse detection that addresses the hardware-compatibility gap directly. The pipeline enriches each function by extracting its existing inline comments, call-graph context, and a project README before embedding it with eight backbone models (MiniLM, MPNet, BGE, E5, GraphCodeBERT, OpenAI text-embedding-3-small, LLaMA 3 8B, StarCoder2 3B) acting as feature extractors. Four hardware-compatibility validators---covering peripheral token overlap, parameter count parity, call-graph dependency overlap, and structural branching pattern---filter candidates directly in the retrieval stack. Evaluated on six public embedded C software projects (184 functions, 4,815 above-plateau pairs), the pipeline reveals that SonarQube produces a 93.6% false-positive rate as a reuse filter, with 83.5% of failures caused by hardware-environment mismatches that static analysis cannot detect. Manual verification of 40 rejected pairs confirms 97.5% validator accuracy, and a diagnostic rule-injection variant identifies the dominant failure categories (McNemar chi-squared~=~294.0, p~$<$~0.001).

</details>


### [141] [AgentForge: An Immersive Role-Playing Platform for Learning Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04148)
*Zihan Fang,Yueke Zhang,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AgentForge，一个沉浸式学习系统，让新手在多智能体代码修复工作流中扮演四个软件工程角色之一（任务规划者、补丁作者、代码审查者或测试运行者），通过角色脚手架和元认知支持来提高新手与Agentic AI协作及批判性评估输出的能力。37名新手开发者参与的研究显示任务完成率较高，但不同实践的交互需求差异显著，代码审查实践最具挑战性，参与者普遍报告对软件修复和智能体协作的理解显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI在软件开发中被广泛用于协调规划、实施、审查和测试，但缺乏决策和交互的透明度。现有系统假设用户能有效引导AI决策并验证其输出，这对新手构成挑战——他们需要同时学习Agentic AI的工作原理、有效协作方式以及批判性评估输出的能力。

Method: 提出了AgentForge系统，让新手在多智能体代码修复工作流中担任四个角色之一，同时AI代理执行其余角色。通过基于角色的脚手架和元认知支持，系统使角色职责清晰化、展示代理协调和中间产物，并鼓励新手指监控和评估自身决策。通过37名新手开发者的研究评估系统效果。

Result: 参与者在AI代理支持下实现了较高的任务完成率。但不同实践的交互需求差异显著：代码审查实践需要更多交互轮次、重定向和完成时间（padj=.004），且被认为最具挑战性。参与者报告对软件修复和智能体协作的理解有显著提升（padj<.001）。

Conclusion: AgentForge能够帮助新手在培养实用软件工程技能的同时，学会更批判性和更有效地与Agentic AI协作，为面向新手的人机协作AI学习提供了有效的支持方案。

Abstract: Agentic AI is increasingly used to coordinate planning, implementation, review, and testing in software development, yet it often offers limited transparency into its decisions and interactions. Many such systems also assume that users can effectively guide the AI's decisions and validate its outputs. This assumption poses a particular challenge for novices, who must simultaneously learn how agentic AI works, how to collaborate with it effectively, and how to evaluate its outputs critically. To address this challenge, we present \textit{AgentForge}, an immersive learning system in which novices take on one of four software-engineering roles: Task Planner, Patch Author, Code Reviewer, or Test Runner, within a multi-agent code-repair workflow. In each practice session, the novices perform their chosen role while AI agents perform the remaining three. Through role-based scaffolding and metacognitive support, AgentForge clarifies role-specific responsibilities, makes agent coordination and intermediate artifacts visible, and encourages novices to monitor and evaluate their decisions. In a study with 37 novice developers, participants achieved high task-completion rates with AI-agent support. However, interaction demands differed significantly across practices: the Code Reviewer practice required more interaction turns, reroutes, and completion time ($p_{\mathrm{adj}} = .004$) and was perceived as the most challenging. Participants nevertheless reported significant gains in their understanding of software repair and agent collaboration ($p_{\mathrm{adj}} < .001$). These findings suggest that AgentForge can help novices develop practical software-engineering skills while learning to collaborate with agentic AI more critically and effectively.

</details>


### [142] [MergeSE: Post-Hoc Model Merging for Software Engineering Tasks Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04181)
*Palash R. Roy,Banani Roy,Kevin A. Schneider,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MergeSE，一个开源的CLI和Web工具，用于HuggingFace编码器checkpoint的免训练模型合并。该工具支持五种合并算法、九种SE分类任务类型，提供tasks/inspect/merge/evaluate/export五种操作，旨在解决代码模型在分布偏移下性能退化的问题，并让SE研究人员无需训练即可合并领域专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调的代码模型往往退化为领域专家，在分布偏移下性能严重下降（如克隆检测中跨语言场景F1下降71%）。虽然有研究表明模型合并可以有效解决这种碎片化问题（达到多任务训练93%的性能，且对未见克隆类型的泛化能力提升4倍），但缺乏实用的工具供SE研究人员进行checkpoint兼容性诊断、专家合并、基准验证和模型导出。

Method: MergeSE是一个开源的CLI和Web工具，包含五种操作（tasks, inspect, merge, evaluate, export），支持五种合并算法（TIES, DARE-TIES, Wudi, PCB, averaging），具备跨任务分类头不匹配检测、可复现的确定性输出、内置基准样本用于冒烟测试。工具面向HuggingFace编码器checkpoint，内置九种SE任务类型注册表（包括漏洞检测、缺陷预测、代码异味检测等）。

Result: 合并两个124M参数的checkpoint在CPU上5秒内完成。端到端验证表明MergeSE生成的checkpoint与参考实现一致，并能从领域专家恢复跨域性能。工具已在线部署（https://mergese.usask.ca），开发仓库公开（https://github.com/srlabUsask/MergeSE）。

Conclusion: MergeSE填补了SE社区缺乏实用的免训练模型合并工具这一空白，为软件工程研究人员提供了从专家模型合并到验证再到部署的完整工作流支持。

Abstract: Fine-tuned code models often behave as domain specialists and can degrade sharply under distribution shift: in our clone-detection setting, a model trained on same-language clones drops 71\% F1 on cross-language clones, while multi-task training falls to 0.151 F1 on unseen AI-generated clones. Our companion study shows that post-hoc model merging can address this fragmentation, achieving 93\% of multi-task performance without training data while generalizing 4$\times$ better to unseen clone types. However, no practical tool exists that lets SE researchers diagnose checkpoint compatibility, merge specialists, validate results on SE benchmarks, and export models for deployment. We present \textbf{MergeSE}, an open-source CLI and web tool for training-free model merging of HuggingFace encoder checkpoints. While motivated by OOD generalization in clone detection, MergeSE supports SE classification workflows more broadly through a built-in registry of nine task types, including vulnerability detection, defect prediction, and code-smell detection. MergeSE provides five operations: \textit{tasks}, \textit{inspect}, \textit{merge}, \textit{evaluate}, and \textit{export}. It supports five merging algorithms, including TIES, DARE-TIES, Wudi, PCB, and averaging; detects cross-task classification-head mismatches; produces seedable deterministic outputs; and includes bundled benchmark samples for smoke-test reproduction. A full merge of two 124M-parameter checkpoints completes in under 5 seconds on CPU. End-to-end validation confirms that MergeSE-produced checkpoints match reference implementations and recover cross-domain performance from domain-specific specialists. The tool is available online at https://mergese.usask.ca, and the development repository is at https://github.com/srlabUsask/MergeSE.

</details>


### [143] [SONAR: Task-Aware Code Summary Evaluation for LLM Consumers Without References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04195)
*Simantika Bhattacharjee Dristi,Matthew B. Dwyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SONAR 是一个无参考的代码摘要评估框架，从正确性、抽象性、简洁性和流畅性四个维度，通过代码再生成的方式来评估摘要对 LLM 消费代理在下游软件工程任务中的实用性，发现正确性和抽象性与 LLM 性能显著相关，而简洁性和流畅性对 LLM 消费者影响不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代码摘要评估从人类开发者视角出发，以与参考摘要的相似度和人类偏好为标准，但忽略了 LLM 工具和代理越来越多地消费代码摘要作为软件工程任务的输入这一现实。什么样的摘要对消费代理真正有用仍未被探索。

Method: 提出 SONAR 无参考评估框架，从四个维度（正确性、抽象性、简洁性、流畅性）评估代码摘要。核心创新在于基于代码再生成的方法：利用摘要重新生成代码，将重建结果作为摘要质量的信号，无需参考摘要或人类/LLM 的主观判断。

Result: 在四个下游 SE 任务中评估后，发现正确性和抽象性与 LLM 性能显著相关（相关性比最好的基线高 14 倍），而简洁性和流畅性对 LLM 消费者影响不显著。对 11 个流行 LLM 的大规模评估揭示了各模型在不同质量维度上的优劣势。

Conclusion: 什么样的代码摘要有用不仅取决于摘要本身的质量，还取决于任务和消费者类型。SONAR 为任务感知的摘要生成研究提供了新的评估范式，并指出了与传统人类偏好相异的 LLM 消费偏好。

Abstract: Source code summaries have traditionally been evaluated from a human developer's perspective, with quality determined by how closely they resemble developer-written references and how well they align with human preferences. But this overlooks a growing reality: LLM-based tools and agents increasingly consume code summaries as inputs for software engineering (SE) tasks, and what makes a summary useful for a consuming agent on a task remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose SONAR, a reference-free framework that evaluates source code summaries along four dimensions: Correctness, Abstraction, Conciseness, and Fluency. Rather than optimizing for a pre-written "gold standard", SONAR introduces a novel code regeneration-based approach that uses a summary to regenerate code and leverages that reconstruction as a quality signal of the summary. This provides an empirical grounding that requires neither a reference summary nor the subjective judgment of humans or LLMs. We evaluate SONAR's dimensions on their ability to influence LLM performance across four downstream SE tasks. We find that Correctness, followed by Abstraction, significantly correlates with LLM performance, with correlations up to 14X higher than the best baseline. Conciseness and Fluency, though widely valued by human developers, remain mostly insignificant to an LLM consumer, suggesting that what makes a summary useful is task- and consumer-dependent. Through a large-scale evaluation of 11 popular LLMs using SONAR, we further identify the strengths and weaknesses of different models across each quality dimension, while offering insights to facilitate future research on task-aware summarization.

</details>


### [144] [A Unified Model for Cross-Domain Clone Detection via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04215)
*Palash R. Roy,Banani Roy,Kevin A. Schneider,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了模型合并（model merging）技术用于构建跨域代码克隆检测器，发现基于共享预训练基座的TIES合并方法能有效创建跨域检测器，达到单任务性能的93%，且在未见过的AI生成克隆上泛化能力优于多任务训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码克隆类型日益多样化（从语法复制到跨语言语义克隆再到AI生成重复代码），导致克隆检测领域碎片化危机。现有深度学习检测器是领域专家，在训练分布外性能严重退化（F1下降超过70%）。部署多个专用模型不切实际，而训练单一跨域检测器需要同时访问所有训练数据。

Method: 系统评估了三种模型合并范式：（1）基于任务向量的参数合并（5种方法）；（2）基于贪心层拼接的结构合并；（3）跨tokenizer对齐。实验涵盖4个代码模型、3个基准、12种配置，涉及同基座合并和跨基座合并两种设置。

Result: 同基座TIES合并可在UniXcoder上达到0.865的联合F1，为多任务性能的93%，且无需合并阶段的训练数据。WUDI在分布内联合F1最高（0.899），但TIES对未见过的AI生成克隆泛化更好。跨基座合并仅获得边际且高方差收益。合并检测器在GPTCloneBench上优于零样本代码LLM，且对未见AI生成克隆的泛化能力比多任务训练高4倍。

Conclusion: 共享预训练基座上的任务向量兼容性是模型合并有效性的关键约束因素。模型合成为构建跨域克隆检测器提供了实用方案，但存在分布内性能与OOD鲁棒性之间的权衡。TIES被推荐为最实用的合并方法。

Abstract: The growing diversity of code clone types, from syntactic copies to cross-language semantic clones to AI-generated duplicates, has created a fragmentation crisis in clone detection. Current deep learning detectors are domain specialists that degrade significantly outside their training distribution, with F1 drops exceeding 70% across domains. Deploying multiple specialized models is impractical, yet training a single cross-domain detector requires simultaneous access to all training data. To address this, we investigate model merging, a family of post-hoc techniques that operate solely on trained checkpoints. We evaluate parameter merging with five task-vector methods, architecture merging via greedy layer stitching, and cross-tokenizer alignment across four code models, three benchmarks, and twelve configurations. Same-base TIES merging creates effective cross-domain detectors, validated across two model families and three random seeds, reaching 0.865 combined F1 on UniXcoder, 93% of multi-task performance without any training data at the merging step. WUDI achieves the highest in-distribution combined F1 at 0.899, but TIES generalizes better to unseen AI-generated clones, making it our recommended method. Cross-base merging yields only marginal and high-variance gains across all five methods, indicating that task vector compatibility through a shared pre-trained base is the binding factor for effective merging. Merged detectors also outperform zero-shot code LLMs on GPTCloneBench at lower inference cost and generalize up to 4x better than multi-task training to unseen AI-generated clones, suggesting a trade-off between in-domain performance and OOD robustness. This work provides one of the first systematic empirical studies of model merging for software engineering and a practical recipe for building cross-domain clone detectors.

</details>


### [145] [Neuro-Symbolic Proof-of-Vulnerability Generation with Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04217)
*Yu Nong,Haipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: POVGEN是一个低成本的神经符号框架，通过语义聚焦和LLM引导的约束推理（使用开源权重模型）来生成Proof-of-Vulnerability (PoV)，在基准测试中达到78.98%的成功率，远超模糊测试和符号执行，并在真实CVE上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件漏洞持续存在，但验证漏洞十分困难：PoV需要具体的触发输入，而公开漏洞往往缺乏此类输入。现有技术在有效性、可扩展性、成本和可控性之间做出不同权衡，需要互补的设计方案。

Method: POVGEN采用三步流程：首先定位漏洞相关区域（可利用补丁信息），然后进行路径敏感的可达性分析，最后通过LLM引导的约束推理结合SMT求解器提取并求解约束来生成PoV。该方法使用开源权重模型，实现本地运行且无按样本API成本。

Result: 在最新基准测试中为78.98%的漏洞成功生成PoV，优于模糊测试（最高50.20%）和符号执行（2.45%）。在250个无公开PoV的真实CVE中，74.80%的情况生成了有效PoV，无补丁信息时复现率65.1%。微调后的开源模型在关键约束推理子任务上匹配前沿商业LLM。生成的PoV揭示了6个有缺陷的补丁和5个此前未报告的漏洞（其中4个已被开发者确认并修复）。

Conclusion: POVGEN通过神经符号方法有效降低了PoV生成的成本和难度，在保持高成功率的同时实现了本地化部署，并能实际发现真实世界中的补丁缺陷和未报告漏洞，具有重要的实践价值。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities are persistent, but validating them remains difficult: a Proof-of-Vulnerability (PoV) requires a concrete input that triggers the vulnerable behavior, yet public triggering inputs are often unavailable for disclosed vulnerabilities. Existing techniques make different tradeoffs in effectiveness, scalability, cost, and controllability, leaving room for complementary designs. To complement them, we present POVGEN, a low-cost neuro-symbolic framework that makes PoV generation cost-effective via semantic focusing and LLM-guided constraint reasoning using open-weight models. POVGEN first localizes vulnerability-relevant regions (utilizing patch information if available), then performs path-sensitive reachability analysis, and finally generates PoVs by extracting and solving constraints with LLM-guided reasoning backed by an SMT solver. POVGEN successfully generates PoVs for 78.98% of vulnerabilities in a recent benchmark, outperforming fuzzing (up to 50.20%) and symbolic execution (2.45%). On 250 real-world CVEs without public PoVs, it generates valid PoVs for 74.80% of cases and reproduces 65.1% when without patch information. The fine-tuned open-weight models match frontier commercial LLMs on key sub-tasks (i.e., the core constraint-reasoning steps) while running locally at no per-sample API cost. Applying the generated PoVs revealed six flawed patches in disclosed CVEs (all subsequently fixed) and five previously unreported vulnerabilities (of which four have been confirmed and fixed by the developers).

</details>


### [146] [CURATE: Leveraging LLM Agents to Compose, Catalog, and Deploy Reproducible Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04270)
*Nolan Cutler,Chia-Chen Kuo,Nanda Velugoti,Kathryn Newhart,Renato Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CURATE系统，一个用户参与的多代理框架，利用LLM代理管理可组合工作流的全生命周期（包括部署和共享），并通过模块目录支持模块的存储与复用，填补了现有编码代理只关注代码生成而忽略工作流全生命周期的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理主要聚焦于代码生成，未能覆盖工作流的完整生命周期（包括部署和共享）。用户仍需独立开发模块并自行管理部署，这增加了工程和科学领域的入门门槛，阻碍了应用和科学工作流的加速开发。

Method: CURATE（Composition, User-in-the-loop, Reuse, and Automated Task Execution）是一个人类参与的多代理系统，利用LLM代理管理并开发可组合工作流的整个生命周期。核心特性是模块目录，支持工作流间模块的存储和复用，可扩展支持FAIR原则，促进精选模块和子图的共享与复用。

Result: 使用Claude Opus 4.8构建初始原型，通过6个实验验证可行性：包括复现和适配4个来自SeBS-Flow基准测试套件的工作流，以及自动化开发和扩展一个用于模拟厌氧消化的环境工程复杂机理模型工作流。

Conclusion: CURATE通过结合人类参与、模块复用和自动化任务执行，实现了工作流从生成到部署再到共享的完整生命周期管理，在应用与科学工作流开发中展现出可行性和潜力。

Abstract: Agentic code generation has shown promise in automating and accelerating software development by utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate, test, and deploy code. For engineers and scientists, such systems have the potential to accelerate the development of applied and scientific workflows while reducing barriers to entry in domains that have yet to fully realize their benefits. However, a key gap remains: existing coding agents primarily focus on code generation and do not address the entire workflow lifecycle, including deployment and sharing. As a result, users develop and stitch modules independently while managing deployment on their own. To address this gap, we propose CURATE - Composition, User-in-the-loop, Reuse, and Automated Task Execution - a novel human-in-the-loop multi-agent system that uses LLM agents to manage and develop composable workflows across their entire lifecycle. A key feature of the system is a catalog that allows for the storage and reuse of modules across workflows. Module catalogs provide a foundation that can be expanded to support FAIR principles by facilitating the sharing and reuse of curated modules and subgraphs. We demonstrate the feasibility of our system with an initial prototype using Claude Opus 4.8, comprising 6 experiments: reproducing and adapting 4 workflows derived from the SeBS-Flow benchmark suite, and automating the development and scaling of a workflow that leverages a complex mechanistic model in environmental engineering used to simulate anaerobic digestion.

</details>


### [147] [LLM-based Vulnerability Discovery in Business Process Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04271)
*Ben Falchuk,Himanshu Garg,Euthimios Panagos,Sioan Zohar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍MIRABELLE系统，该系统利用AI/ML技术从业务流程文档（如ISO 9000/9001文档、用户指南、工作说明和流程执行日志）中识别业务逻辑漏洞。论文重点实验了多种大语言模型（LLMs）在漏洞检测关键阶段的表现，包括短语句中的语法与技术错误标记，以及完整流程结构恢复与提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务流程与软件和硬件一样，容易受到漏洞影响，可能导致产品质量问题、延迟和成本增加。业务流程漏洞可能源于需求冲突、文档歧义、无效测量规范、质量检查遗漏或实现与规范不一致等多种因素，因此需要自动化工具来识别这些漏洞。

Method: MIRABELLE系统处理可用的业务流程文档，生成业务逻辑的属性图表示，结合图方法和形式逻辑方法识别潜在漏洞。其中，利用大语言模型（LLMs）从自然语言流程文档中提取业务逻辑（如操作执行序列、决策、输入/输出资源），并在多个关键阶段进行实验评估。

Result: 论文报告了多种LLMs在漏洞检测关键阶段（从短语句的语法和技术错误标记，到完整流程结构恢复与提取）的性能表现。

Conclusion: 从自然语言文档中提取业务逻辑面临领域专业知识要求、流程固有复杂性和信息量巨大的挑战，本文通过LLM实验探索了这些挑战的解决方案，验证了LLMs在MIRABELLE系统中的作用。

Abstract: Just like software and hardware, business processes are susceptible to vulnerabilities that can lead to product quality issues, delays, and increased costs. Business process vulnerabilities can arise from a variety of sources, including conflicting requirements, ambiguous documentation, invalid measurement spec-ifications, omission of quality checks, or implementations that differ from speci-fications. MIRABELLE is a system that identifies and characterizes business logic (BL) vulnerabilities from available business process representations, in-cluding ISO 9000/9001 documentation, user guides, work instructions, and pro-cess execution logs. MIRABELLE leverages recent advances in AI/ML to pro-cess available business process documentation and generate attributed graph rep-resentations of the business logic that can be processed using both graph and for-mal logic approaches for identifying potential vulnerabilities. However, extract-ing the business logic (e.g., operation execution sequences, decisions, input/out-put resources) from mostly natural language artifacts is challenging due to the required domain expertise, inherent process complexity, and the sometimes very large volumes of information. This paper focuses on our experimentation with Large Language Models (LLMs) and their role within MIRABELLE. We report on the performance of several LLMs across vital stages of vulnerability detection, from grammatical and technical error-flagging in short phrasings, to complete process structure recovery and extraction.

</details>


### [148] [EA-Graph: Artifact-Anchored Verification Memory for Coding Agents under Upstream Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04278)
*Hwai-Jung Hsu,Cheng-Jan Chi,Hanna Everett*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EA-Graph，一种以工件为锚的记忆系统，用于存储编码代理的验证声明。它以子路径粒度表示工件，将声明锚定到建立声明的内容上，并区分证据强度与新鲜度。在7个生成仓库、42个会话、两种模型层级的实验中，工件锚定记忆在小模型（Haiku）上一致优于散文笔记和无持久记忆（所有p=0.0156）；在大模型（Sonnet）上达到完美但统计不显著。没有会话虚构被扣留的内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理跨会话工作时，散文笔记只能保留结论而丢失支撑结论的程序状态。上游变更后，仓库仍可构建，但先前的验证声明可能已失效。现有方法无法判断声明是否仍然有效，因此需要一种以工件为锚的记忆机制。

Method: EA-Graph采用工件锚定记忆方案：以子路径粒度表示工件、将别名解析到叶子定义、将每个验证声明锚定到用于建立该声明的内容、并保持证据强度与新鲜度分离。当替换内容不可用时，声明被标记为"不可证明"而非猜测。

Result: 实验覆盖7个干净世界、14个模型世界实例、3种记忆条件和2个模型层级的42个会话。在Haiku层，工件锚定记忆在全部7个世界中胜出（配对Wilcoxon检验均p=0.0156）；在Sonnet层锚定条件达到完美但对照组天花板效应导致预注册对比不显著。所有会话均未虚构被扣留的内容。

Conclusion: 工件锚定记忆能提升较小模型对声明可证明性的判断能力，可能通过外化会话内重新推导来缩小模型间能力差距，但不建立跨模型等价性。研究不涉及效率或修复质量的声明。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly work across sessions, but prose notes can preserve a conclusion without the program state that supported it. After an upstream change, a repository may still build even though earlier verification claims are no longer valid. EA-Graph is an artifact-anchored memory for verification claims. It represents artifacts at sub-path granularity, resolves aliases to leaf definitions, anchors each claim to the content used to establish it, and keeps evidence strength separate from freshness. When replacement content is unavailable, the claim becomes unprovable rather than guessed.
  EA-Graph is evaluated on generated repositories whose behavior-to-artifact ground truth is known by construction. The task is to classify prior claims as unaffected, affected, or unprovable after value drift, logic drift, and deliberately withheld upstream content. The analysis covers 42 sessions across seven clean worlds, 14 model-world instances, three memory conditions, and two model tiers. In the Haiku round, artifact-anchored memory outscored prose notes and no persistent memory in all seven worlds; each exact paired Wilcoxon comparison yielded p = 0.0156. In the Sonnet round, the anchored condition was perfect, but frequent control ceilings left the preregistered contrasts non-significant. No session fabricated withheld content. These results support a bounded claim: artifact-anchored memory improved the smaller model's provability judgments in this testbed. An exploratory comparison further suggests that structured claim memory may narrow a capability gap by externalizing in-session re-derivation, but it does not establish cross- model equivalence. The study makes no claim about efficiency or repair quality.

</details>


### [149] [COMPAS: Difficulty-Aware Joint Search for Optimizing Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04336)
*Jingzhi Gong,Jie M. Zhang,Gunel Jahangirova,Dong Huang,Mohammad Reza Mousavi,Mark Harman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: COMPAS是一种对代码生成系统进行联合优化的方法，同时考虑模型、提示词和解码设置三者的交互，通过学习面向不同难度的质量-成本前沿，在线为每个测试任务路由匹配配置，在LiveCodeBench上将pass@1从45.9%提升至52.8%并将成本从$36.57降至$4.92，同时在SWE-bench上从70.0%提升至76.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成系统的优化方法通常只优化部分组件（如仅选模型、仅调提示词）或对所有任务使用同一固定配置，忽视了模型、提示词和解码设置三者之间的联合及分组特定交互，导致性能次优。

Method: COMPAS是一种难度感知方法，通过低成本的模型选择与联合提示词-解码设置搜索，学习针对不同任务难度分组的质量-成本前沿，然后在线上将每个测试任务路由到匹配的前沿，无需额外搜索。该方法基于三个观察：提示词与解码设置存在交互、调优效果因模型而异、最优配置随任务难度变化。

Result: 在匹配的搜索预算下，COMPAS在LiveCodeBench上从最佳基线的45.9% pass@1提升到52.8%，成本从$36.57降至$4.92；在仓库级代码生成基准SWE-bench上，任务解决率从70.0%提升到76.0%。

Conclusion: COMPAS证明了联合优化模型、提示词与解码设置并采用难度感知分组路由，可以显著提升代码生成的质量-成本权衡，且方法可迁移到仓库级代码生成任务。

Abstract: Code generation systems make each LLM call with a model, a prompt, and decoding settings. However, existing optimization methods usually tune only part of these choices or use one fixed configuration for all tasks: global optimizers search one configuration for all tasks, routers choose only a model, and prompt optimizers keep the model and decoding settings fixed. This leaves their joint, group-specific interactions unclear. We therefore examine how these choices interact and observe that prompts and decoding settings interact, tuning effects vary by model, and the best configuration varies by task difficulty. Guided by these observations, we introduce COMPAS (Code-generation Optimization over Models, Prompts, And Decoding Settings), a difficulty-aware method that learns group-specific quality-cost fronts through low-cost model selection and joint prompt-decoding search, then routes each test task to its matching front online without further search. Under a matched search budget on LiveCodeBench, COMPAS improves pass@1 from 45.9% for the best baseline to 52.8% while reducing cost from $36.57 to $4.92. This also transfers to repository-level code generation on SWE-bench, resolving 76.0% of tasks versus 70.0% for the best baseline. Code and the reproducibility artifact are available at https://github.com/gjz78910/COMPAS.

</details>


### [150] [ExeCRE: Execution-Consistency Guided Reliability Estimation for Self-Correcting Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04439)
*Yiru Dong,Richong Zhang,Fanshuang Kong,Si Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ExeCRE框架，通过统计执行输出的一致性模式来估计代码可靠性，替代依赖不可靠监督信号的自我修正管道，在代码生成和数学推理任务上均显著提升效果与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代码执行反馈的自我修正管道依赖监督信号（如测试用例或LLM反馈），但此类信号可靠性未知，可能导致误导性反馈、不必要的修订及错误最终答案，影响代码生成效果。

Method: ExeCRE不通过测试或LLM反馈判断候选代码，而是对大量随机生成输入收集代码执行输出，将其投影为一致性信号，并应用Dawid-Skene模型推断潜在代码可靠性，再将该可靠性估计集成到自我修正管道中。

Result: 在GPT-5.2和LiveCodeBench上，正确代码上的误导性反馈数量从基线113.2降到14.0；同时该方法在代码数学推理任务上也观察到类似收益，显著提升有效性和稳定性。

Conclusion: ExeCRE通过执行一致性统计推断代码可靠性，减少了误导性修正信号，使生成代码在基于执行的管道中得到更可靠的利用，并具有跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made notable progress in code generation, but they still struggle on challenging tasks that require sophisticated algorithms or complex implementations. Recent methods increasingly use code execution as feedback, especially in self-correction pipelines that construct verification signals from generated code. However, these pipelines often depend on supervision signals whose reliability is unknown, which can introduce misleading feedback, unnecessary revisions, and incorrect final answers. To address this issue, we propose ExeCRE, an Execution-Consistency guided code Reliability Estimation framework. Instead of judging candidate code by tests or LLM feedback, ExeCRE estimates code reliability by statistically analyzing consistency patterns in execution outputs over a large number of randomly generated inputs. It collects execution outputs over generated inputs, projects them into consistency signals, and applies the Dawid-Skene model to infer latent code reliability. We integrate ExeCRE into self-correction for code generation. Experiments show that ExeCRE consistently improves both effectiveness and stability, while substantially reducing misleading correction signals. Under GPT-5.2 on LiveCodeBench, the average number of misleading feedback cases on already correct code drops from 113.2 with a representative self-correction baseline to 14.0 with ExeCRE. As an additional study, we apply the same reliability estimation strategy to code-based mathematical reasoning and observe similar benefits. These results suggest that ExeCRE enables more reliable use of generated code in execution-based pipelines.

</details>


### [151] [AdaptAgent: A Multi-agent, Domain-Guided Reasoning Framework for Code Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04459)
*Xiaokai Rong,Hridya Dhulipala,Aashish Yadavally,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptAgent，一个多智能体、领域引导的推理框架，用于解决代码适配问题——即将LLM生成的代码或在线代码片段正确地集成到目标代码库中。AdaptAgent通过分解任务为多个专门化智能体（意图总结器、策略智能体、领域规划器、上下文挖掘器和代码适配器），配合编译器验证器迭代精炼，在真实数据集上优于强基线，并生成与开发者实际适配模式一致的补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者经常需要将LLM生成的代码或在线论坛中的代码片段集成到自己的项目中，但这一过程目前主要依赖人工，不仅繁琐且容易出错。成功集成远非简单复制粘贴，需要用户在目标仓库的指定位置生成正确的适配性修改。现有方法缺乏对此问题的系统性形式化和自动化解决方案，因此需要专门针对代码适配问题的框架。

Method: AdaptAgent将代码适配形式化为一个明确定义的问题，采用多智能体、领域引导的推理框架。该框架将适配任务分解为五个通过类型化工件通信的专门化智能体：(1) Intent Summarizer从Q&A文本中提取适配目标；(2) Policy Agent从六个适配类别中派生领域策略；(3) Domain Planner生成自排序的计划；(4) Context Miner从目标代码库中提炼兄弟方法语义；(5) Code Adapter将计划实现为最小的统一差异补丁。此外，使用基于编译器的验证器进行迭代精炼。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，AdaptAgent在语义正确性方面优于强基线方法，生成的补丁能够反映开发者的实际适配模式。消融研究证明每个智能体都是必要的，其中规划对代码加固和异常处理尤为关键，意图提取对逻辑定制至关重要。

Conclusion: AdaptAgent通过专业化的多智能体分工和领域策略引导，有效解决了代码适配问题，验证了将复杂适配任务分解为协作智能体并结合编译器验证迭代精炼的框架设计的有效性，为自动化代码集成提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Developers often need to adapt into their projects the code generated from LLMs or code snippets from online forums. However, integrating them into an existing repository remains challenging in a manual process. A successful integration typically requires more than copying code as a user must produce correct adapting changes at a designated location in the target repository. We formalize this as the code adaptation problem: given a snippet, functional intent, a target repository, and an adaptation location, generate a patch that adapts the snippet into the repository. We present AdaptAgent, a multi-agent, domain-guided reasoning framework for code adaptation. Rather than relying on single-shot prompting, AdaptAgent decomposes adaptation into specialized agents that communicate via typed artifacts: an Intent Summarizer extracts adaptation goals from Q&A text; a Policy Agent derives domain policies from six adaptation categories; a Domain Planner generates a self-ordered plan; a Context Miner distills sibling-method semantics from the target codebase; and a Code Adapter realizes the plan as a minimal unified diff, iteratively refined using a compiler-based Verifier. This division of labor enables robust, policy-aligned adaptations and supports adapting code snippets into a project. On a real-world dataset, AdaptAgent outperforms strong baselines in semantic correctness and produces patches that mirror developers' actual adaptation patterns. Our ablation study shows each agent's necessity, especially planning for code-hardening and exception-handling, and intent for logic customization.

</details>


### [152] [Energy Efficient AI-Enabled Wireless Sensor Networks for Mission Critical Environments: A Systematic Review across Smart Grid, AI, and Urban Infrastructure Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04499)
*Alexandros Gazis,Valeri Mladenov,Kleanthi SantamourI,Stylianos Pappas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2023至2026年间AI赋能无线传感器网络（WSNs）在关键任务环境（如电力电子和城市基础设施系统）中的能效研究。作者从50篇DOI索引研究中提炼出AI提升WSN能耗行为的多条技术路径，并强调能效不能孤立优化，而应与安全、延迟和可靠性等需求协同考虑，未来应聚焦轻量级、可解释、安全且经现场验证的AI驱动WSN架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进无线传感器网络在连续监测、自主运行、可靠通信和快速决策等场景中的应用需求日益增长，而能源效率是制约其部署的关键瓶颈。现有研究中能效往往被作为孤立的优化目标，忽略了与安全、延迟和可靠性等关键任务要求之间的紧密耦合关系，因此需要系统性地梳理AI技术如何在实际关键场景中提升WSN能效。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，从2023至2026年的文献中筛选出50篇满足纳入标准的DOI索引研究，对其进行定性的主题编码和比较分析。其余参考文献仅作为历史、方法学或技术背景引用，不纳入综述语料库。

Result: 研究发现AI可通过多条途径改善WSN能耗行为，包括路由与聚类优化、边缘AI、强化学习、模糊逻辑、元启发式优化以及基于AI的安全机制。同时研究揭示，在关键任务系统中能效、安全、延迟和可靠性是紧密关联的相互制约需求，不能将能效视为孤立性能指标。

Conclusion: 未来研究应摒弃孤立优化协议的传统思路，转向构建轻量级、可解释、安全且经现场验证的AI驱动WSN架构，以适应真实运行环境中的关键任务需求。

Abstract: Advanced wireless sensor networks powered by artificial intelligence are increasingly required for applications demanding continuous monitoring, autonomous operation, reliable communication, and fast decision support. This systematic review examines recent work from 2023 to 2026 on energy-efficient, AI-enabled wireless sensor networks (WSNs) in mission-critical environments, with particular focus on power electronics, and urban infrastructure systems. The authors synthesise a corpus of 50 DOI indexed studies satisfying inclusion criteria that received qualitative thematic coding and comparative analysis. Other references were only cited to provide historical, methodological, or technical context and were not included in the systematic review corpus. As such, our results show that AI can improve WSN energy behaviour through routing and clustering, edge AI, reinforcement learning, fuzzy logic, metaheuristic optimisation, and AI-based security. At the same time, energy efficiency cannot be treated as an isolated performance target. In mission-critical systems, security, latency, and reliability are closely interlinked requirements. The review concludes that future work should move away from optimising protocols in isolation, and instead focus on building lightweight, explainable, secure, and field-tested AI-driven WSN architectures suited to real operational environments.

</details>


### [153] [The Order Is the Guarantee: Verifier-Budgeted Code Deletion with Static-First Learned Proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04611)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Han Wang,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DELSCOUT将冗余代码删除问题形式化为"提议调度"问题：排序器对单条语句删除候选进行排序，执行套件验证并通过的候选被接受，预算限制测试次数。核心洞察是候选排序（而非模型置信度）才是部署可推理的控制面。通过在MBPP上使用0.5B/0.6B/8B排序器的实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码模型在编程基准上已匹配或超越人类，但基准成功不意味着可维护软件。提示驱动的"vibe coding"是增量式的——新分支、守卫和回退逻辑的累积速度快于过时逻辑的移除。因此需要研究在有限执行验证能力下，AI系统应如何正确地"删除"代码（逆向问题）。

Method: 将冗余代码减少形式化为提议调度：一个排序器对单语句删除候选进行排序，执行套件接受第一个通过的候选，预算限制可测试的候选数量。DELSCOUT实现两种调度策略：(1) 有代表性目标域验证时，五槽预算分配三个槽给确定性最短优先候选，两个给互补的学排序器候选；(2) 无验证时，先评估完整静态前缀，再附加学排序器候选，保证覆盖率与字符缩减单调不减。

Result: 在九个MBPP复现（使用0.5B、0.6B和8B排序器）中，混合调度相比匹配的静态基线在验证删除覆盖率上提高9.5%（额外接受6.7个任务），同时消耗更少的验证器调用。无验证场景下覆盖率与字符缩减按构造保证非递减，但验证器调用增加4.8%-62.5%。MBPP+证实域内优势被消除，表明调度控制搜索，而测试套件单独决定"保持行为"的含义。

Conclusion: 最终形成可审计的职责分工：模型拓宽可删除代码的搜索空间，排序约束错误提议可能造成的损害范围，执行保留对每次删除的最终权威。证明了候选排序而非模型置信度才是部署可控的控制面。

Abstract: Frontier coding models now match or exceed strong human reference points on programming benchmarks, yet benchmark success does not imply maintainable software. Prompt-driven "vibe coding" is additive: new branches, guards, and fallbacks accumulate faster than obsolete logic is removed. We study the inverse problem-how an Al system should remove code when execution-verification capacity is finite. We formulate redundant-code reduction as proposal scheduling: a ranker orders single-statement deletion candidates, an execution suite accepts the first candidate that passes, and a budget bounds how many candidates may be tested. Our central observation is that candidate order, not model confidence, is the control surface a deployment can reason about. DELSCOUT instantiates two schedules. Given representative target-domain validation, a five-slot budget spends three slots on deterministic shortest-first candidates and two on complementary learned candidates; across nine MBPP replications with 0.5B, 0.6B, and 8B rankers this raises verified-deletion coverage by 9.5% relative (+6.7 accepted tasks) while consuming slightly fewer verifier calls than the matched static baseline. Without such validation the same rankers can lose coverage under shift, so we instead evaluate the complete static prefix first and append learned candidates only afterwards; for a deterministic verifier this makes coverage and character reduction non-decreasing by construction, at a measured 4.8-62.5% increase in verifier calls. MBPP+ then erases the in-domain advantage, showing that scheduling governs search while the test suite alone governs what "preserving behavior" means. The result is an auditable division of labor: models widen the search for removable code, order bounds the damage a mis-ranked proposal can do, and execution retains authority over every committed deletion.

</details>


### [154] [An Exploratory Study of Agent Plans for Agentic AI Coding Tools in Open-Source Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04661)
*Muhammad Auwal Abubakar,Seyedmoein Mohsenimofidi,Jai Lal Lulla,Jie M. Zhang,Christoph Treude,Sebastian Baltes,Matthias Galster*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开源软件仓库中的Agent Plans（代理计划）文件进行了探索性研究。通过对36,710个GitHub仓库进行筛选，识别出10个仓库中的85个Markdown计划文件，分析了这些文件如何被保留、支持哪些开发活动以及提供何种执行指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注仓库级共享上下文文件（如AGENTS.md）对代理AI编码工具的指导作用，但对更面向任务导向的Agent Plans（代理计划）文件了解甚少，因此需要对其展开专门研究。

Method: 对36,710个GitHub仓库（属于工程化软件项目）进行筛查，最终从10个仓库中识别出85个Markdown格式的Agent Plan文件，并对其内容进行人工分析，考察其保留方式、支持的软件开发活动类型及其提供的执行指导信息。

Result: Agent Plans支持多种软件工程活动，包括维护、设计、构建、质量相关工作及流程支持。最常见的任务导向执行指导包括：实施步骤、具体文件和位置信息、以及测试与验证信息。整体而言，这些工具特定目录下的Agent Plans是一类规模较小但信息丰富的工件，可用于研究人机协作工作流中的任务意图与执行指导。

Conclusion: 仓库中保存的Agent Plans虽数量稀缺且高度集中，但作为研究人类与代理AI在任务意图和执行指导方面协作的工件，具有重要的研究价值。

Abstract: Repository-level configuration artifacts allow developers to provide guidance for agentic AI coding tools, such as Claude Code, Gemini, etc. Although prior research has examined repository-shared context files that capture project-level instructions and conventions (e.g., AGENTS.md files), little is known about more task-oriented artifacts such as Agent Plans. We present an exploratory study of Agent Plans in open-source software repositories, examining how plan files are preserved, which development activities they support, and what information they provide to guide agent execution. We screened 36,710 GitHub repositories belonging to engineered software projects and identified 85 Markdown plan files from 10 repositories. Within this highly concentrated corpus, Agent Plans supported several kinds of software engineering work, including maintenance, design, construction, quality-related work, and process support. They also provided task-oriented execution guidance, most commonly through implementation steps, concrete files and locations, and testing and validation information. Overall, repository-preserved Agent Plans under these tool-specific directories appear to be a narrow but informative artifact for studying task intent and execution guidance in human-agent workflows.

</details>


### [155] [Active-SWE: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Proactive Bug Fixing without Issue Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04682)
*Haobin Li,Ping Deng,Weizhong Qian,Liang Jiang,Zhenyu Huang,Mouxing Yang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Active-SWE基准，用于评估编码智能体在没有issue报告引导的情况下主动发现并修复多个bug的能力，涵盖1,663个任务、六种bug类别和八种语言。实验表明现有最先进的编码智能体在主动缺陷修复任务上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SWE基准通常假设高质量、信息详细的issue报告总是可用，但实际中报告获取和整理复杂度高，这一假设经常被违背。因此需要一个能够评估编码智能体主动发现和修复bug能力的新基准。

Method: 提出Active-SWE基准，包含双轨评估框架和困难度感知的任务构建流水线，将评估从传统的响应式bug修复转向主动式bug修复，场景覆盖多bug修复和潜在bug发现。涵盖1,663个任务、六种bug类别和八种语言。

Result: 广泛实验表明，大多数最先进的编码智能体在主动缺陷修复任务上表现有限：在定位和修复已记录bug、处理多bug修复场景以及发现有效潜在bug方面均表现不佳。

Conclusion: 现有编码智能体在主动式bug修复能力上存在显著不足，Active-SWE基准为该方向的研究提供了更全面的评估平台，为未来发展主动缺陷定位与修复的智能体提供了方向指引。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted in software engineering (SWE) scenarios, capable of fixing a specific bug in large-scale codebase. However, existing SWE benchmarks typically assume that high-quality issue reports with detailed information are always available, which is easily violated in practice due to the complexity of report acquisition and curation. To address this, we introduce Active-SWE, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on proactively discovering and fixing multiple bugs without report guidance, covering 1,663 tasks across six bug categories and eight languages. Beyond shifting the focus from existing reactive bug fixing to proactive bug fixing, Active-SWE enables a more in-depth evaluation by expanding the scope from fixing a specific recorded bug to multiple-bug fixing and potential bug discovery scenarios. To construct Active-SWE, we propose a novel difficulty-aware task formulation pipeline with a dual-track evaluation framework, facilitating comprehensive evaluation of proactive bug-fixing capability. Extensive experiments reveal that most state-of-the-art coding agents struggle with proactive bug-fixing tasks, demonstrating limited performance in locating and resolving recorded bugs, handling multiple bug fixing scenarios, and discovering valid potential bugs.

</details>


### [156] [What We Observe as LLM Behavior Can Be a Side-effect of Inference Backend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04714)
*Shahed Masoudian,Passant Shafaei,Monorama Swain,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了推理框架（如HuggingFace、vLLM、Ollama）对模型基准测试分数的影响，发现后端选择是一个不可忽视的因素，即使在贪婪解码下也能显著改变模型性能，因此建议在报告基准分数时披露后端、版本及完整生成配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数通常被视为模型自身的属性，但产生这些分数的推理框架（后端）被视为无影响因素，其名称和版本几乎从不披露。作者质疑这一假设，希望探究推理框架的选择对模型输出的实际影响程度。

Method: 采用全交叉实验设计：3个指令微调模型 × 5个推理框架 × 6个基准测试 × 4种生成模式，系统考察不同工具（包装器/后端）对基准分数的影响，以及分数变化如何受生成超参数影响。通过方差分解将可变性归因于后端、采样噪声和默认生成参数。

Result: 后端是一个不可忽视的因素：即使在无采样噪声的贪婪解码下，更换后端也能显著改变模型性能，且该效应是结构性的、高度依赖模型。在开箱即用的设置中，约39%的分数变异性源于后端，其余源于采样噪声和各框架的默认生成参数。这些差异在事实性基准上比在社会偏见基准上更为显著。

Conclusion: 基准分数并非与后端无关。作者建议在报告基准分数时披露后端及其版本、完整生成配置，并采用确定性解码以进行跨后端比较，从而保证基准结果的可复现性和可比性。

Abstract: Benchmark scores are reported as properties of a model, yet the inference framework used to produce them, such as HuggingFace, vLLM, or Ollama, are considered non-influential and their names and versions are almost never disclosed. In this work we investigate how much this choice can influence the model output. In a fully-crossed study (three instruction-tuned models x five inference frameworks x six benchmarks x four generation modes) we investigate how different tools (wrappers/backend) influence benchmark scores and how their score changes is influenced by generation hyper-parameters. We find backend to be a non-negligible factor where even under greedy, sampling-noise-free decoding, changing the backend can significantly alter models performance and this effect is structural and strongly model-dependent. Decomposing the variance according to generation mode reveal that considerable portion of the variability (roughly 39\%) a practitioner sees out-of-the-box can stem from the backend, while the remaining stems from sampling noise and each framework's default generation parameters, both of which are avoidable by disclosing and matching the generation configuration. These divergences are more pronounced on factual than on social-bias benchmarks. Overall, benchmark numbers are not backend-agnostic therefore, we recommend disclosing the backend, its version, and the full generation configuration, also using deterministic decoding for cross-backend comparison.

</details>


### [157] [RepoProbe: Benchmarking Architecture-Aware Repository Comprehension with Checklists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04783)
*Yuexi Yang,Alyssa Wu,Ji Luo,Richeng Xuan,Zhichao Hu,Yuhong Liu,Zhen Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReproProbe是一个用于评估大语言模型仓库级代码理解能力的新型基准，基于GitHub Discussions的开放式架构问题，并采用清单式验证协议替代传统标量评分，以更客观地评估模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖GitHub Issues的缺陷报告，导致模型可通过错误日志的模式匹配绕过真正的代码理解，从而低估了"编辑偏差"（模型过早提出代码修改而非理解仓库架构）；同时，现有的LLM-as-a-Judge标量评分存在高方差和低可解释性问题。

Method: 引入RepoProbe基准，使用GitHub Discussions中的开放式架构问答进行仓库级代码理解评估；提出基于清单的验证协议，将答案分解为原子可验证事实，将主观评分替换为客观验证。

Result: 对SOTA LLM的评估揭示了清晰度与基于证据的技术正确性之间存在持续差距；定量确认了编辑偏差的普遍存在，即模型优先生成代码而非进行架构分析；验证协议相比传统标量评分显著提高了评估可靠性。

Conclusion: RepoProbe和清单式验证协议为仓库级代码理解评估提供了更准确、可靠的方法，为未来LLM在软件工程领域的能力评估提供了新的范式。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into software engineering has shifted the focus from function-level generation to repository-scale assistance. However, existing benchmarks largely rely on bug reports from GitHub Issues, which often allow models to bypass genuine understanding via pattern matching on error logs. This misalignment under-measures Edit Bias, which refers to premature generation, where models prematurely propose code modifications instead of understanding the existing repository architecture. Furthermore, current LLM-as-a-Judge scalar scoring suffers from high variance and low interpretability. This work introduces RepoProbe, a novel benchmark for evaluating repository-level code understanding through open-ended Q&A using GitHub Discussions, which focuses on open-ended architectural inquiries rather than defect reporting. To ensure rigorous evaluation, we propose a Checklist-Based Verification Protocol that decomposes answers into atomic, verifiable facts, thereby replacing subjective ratings with objective verification. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art (SOTA) LLMs reveals a persistent gap between high clarity and evidencegrounded technical correctness. It also quantitatively confirms the prevalence of edit bias, in which models prioritize code generation instead of architectural analysis. Finally, we demonstrate that our verification protocol significantly improves evaluation reliability compared to traditional evaluations with scalar scoring.

</details>


### [158] [Scrouting: Cost-Aware Routing of Coding Agents by Scouting the Repository First](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04804)
*Ishaan Bhola,Adithyan Krishnan,Mukunda NS*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SuperScout是一个新一代的仓库级软件问题求解路由系统。与传统基于问题文本的路由器不同，SuperScout先让一个7B规模的"侦察"模型（SuperScout-7B）探索代码仓库，生成结构化交接（handoff），其调用声明经过沙箱验证并剥离虚假声明。随后，基于侦察模型隐藏状态和任务文本的简历式路由器将任务分配到四个前沿修复器之一。在SWE-bench Pro的Python子集（266个任务）上，SuperScout匹配最优单模型的解决率（159 vs 158），但每次求解成本仅为其五分之一。值得注意的是，无路由器消融实验（始终使用最便宜的修复器加交接）与路由系统持平，表明交接而非路由决策是性能的主要来源。配对校准研究揭示，交接的作用是重新分配而非新增解决能力：提升三个较便宜的修复器、略微损害最强修复器，但N=99时各修复器效果仅为方向性。侦察模型的隐藏状态改善了成本路由，而交接文本本身不起作用。侦察模型每任务计算成本不到半美分GPU时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型能够解决仓库级软件问题，但每次尝试成本高昂，且现有路由器仅从问题文本中选择模型，忽略了仓库内部信息的价值。作者旨在通过先侦察仓库再路由的方式，在降低成本的同时保持甚至提升求解性能。

Method: SuperScout包含两个核心组件：(1) SuperScout-7B侦察器，先探索仓库生成结构化交接，其可复现声明经沙箱验证并剥离虚假内容；(2) 基于侦察器隐藏状态和任务文本的简历式路由器，将任务路由到四个前沿修复器之一。新修复器的加入无需重新训练。

Result: 在SWE-bench Pro Python子集（266任务）官方预算档位下，SuperScout达到159/266的解决率，超过最佳单模型的158/266，而每次求解总成本约为其五分之一。但无路由器消融（始终选最便宜修复器加交接）与路由系统打平，说明交接才是性能核心。配对校准研究显示交接重新分配而非新增能力，且侦察器隐藏状态比交接文本更有助于成本路由。

Conclusion: SuperScout证明了先侦察后路由的范式有效，但关键发现是交接信息本身而非路由决策带来了主要收益。交接重新分配了各修复器的求解能力，而侦察器隐藏状态可用于更好的成本感知路由。系统具有高度的可扩展性（新增修复器无需重训），且侦察成本极低（每任务不到半美分GPU时间）。

Abstract: Frontier language models can resolve repository-level software issues, but each attempt is expensive, and existing routers select a model from the issue text alone. We present SuperScout, which routes after scouting the repository: a 7B searcher, SuperScout-7B, first explores the repository and produces a structured handoff whose reproduction claims are sandbox-verified, with false claims stripped before delivery. The searcher's hidden states, together with the task text, then feed a resume-based router that dispatches the task to one of four frontier fixers. Adding a new fixer requires no retraining. On the full Python slice of SWE-bench Pro (266 tasks) under the benchmark's official capped budget tier, SuperScout matches the best single model's solve rate (159 of 266 for SuperScout, 158 for the best model) at about a fifth of the total cost per solve, and the reported configuration sits above the random traffic-splitting baseline. A no-router ablation, always the cheapest fixer with the handoff, ties the routed system on this benchmark, so the handoff rather than the routing decision carries the result. A paired calibration study points to the mechanism: the handoff appears to redistribute rather than add solving ability, lifting the three cheaper fixers while slightly hurting the strongest, though at $N=99$ the per-fixer effects are directional only; the searcher's hidden states improve cost routing on the calibration labels while the handoff's own text does not. The searcher's compute adds less than half a cent of GPU time per task.

</details>


### [159] [A Chain Is Only as Strong as Its Weakest Link: A Scoping Review of System Integration Audits in AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04921)
*Leah Davis,Dominic Martin,AJung Moon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述（扫描4,259篇文献，纳入58篇）发现，将系统集成作为核心评估理念的AI审计尚处于新兴但碎片化的阶段——现有措施很少针对集成特定风险，在满足传统审计期望方面存在巨大差距，且信息与资源的可及性显著影响审计设计。集成审计可归类为三个层面（组件间、系统-环境、多系统），承担风险探索、风险判定、协调和程序规范性等功能，评估内容包括兼容性、完整性和监督等系统集成特有属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统被集成到各种接口和应用中，仅依赖模型为中心的审计不足以应对系统组件与环境交互所产生的风险。系统集成在航空航天等安全关键领域的软件审计中早已是核心要素，但在AI审计中其作用尚未得到充分探索，因此需要系统梳理现有研究现状。

Method: 采用范围综述（scoping review）方法，对4,259篇文献进行扫描筛选，最终纳入58篇以系统集成为核心评估理念的AI审计研究。运用反思性主题分析（reflexive thematic analysis）对这些审计的元素、参与者、促成因素和约束条件进行分析。

Result: 研究发现该领域仍是新兴但碎片化的审计形式：现有措施很少针对集成特定风险；在满足传统审计期望方面存在较大差距；信息与资源的可及性显著影响审计设计。集成审计可在三个层面进行分类（组件间、系统-环境、多系统），具有风险探索、风险判定、协调和程序规范性四项功能，并评估兼容性、完整性和监督等系统集成特有属性。

Conclusion: 该综述呼吁AI社区将系统集成作为应对AI风险的核心策略，并开发能够捕获超出组件级评估范畴的、跨组件/环境/系统故障的审计实践。

Abstract: As AI systems become increasingly integrated into diverse interfaces and applications, model-centric audits are insufficient to address risks arising from interactions among system components and deployment environments. System integration has long been central to software audits in safety-critical domains such as aerospace. However, its role in AI auditing remains underexplored. Scanning through 4,259 documents, we present a scoping review of AI audits that treat system integration as a core tenet of evaluation (n = 58). Using reflexive thematic analysis, we analyze their elements, actors, enablers, and constraints. We find that the corpus represents an emerging yet still fragmented form of AI auditing: few existing measures target integration-specific risks; large gaps remain in meeting traditional audit expectations; and access to necessary information and resources significantly influences audit design. Nonetheless, integration can be categorized across three sites (inter-component, system-environment, and multi-system), each serving the functions of risk exploration, risk determination, coordination, and procedural regularity. Deviating from other types of evaluations, these audits assess qualities specific to system integration, including compatibility, completeness, and oversight. This review calls on the AI community to prioritize system integration as a core strategy for addressing AI risk, and to develop audit practices capable of capturing failures across components, environments, and systems beyond the reach of component-level evaluation.

</details>


### [160] [SciCode-Verified: How Benchmark Defects Underestimated the Scientific-Coding Ability of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04975)
*Sihan Hu,Lyuhan Huang,Youjin Deng,Kun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现科学编码基准SciCode存在严重缺陷：对65个测试问题的领域专家审计发现263个缺陷，其中192个（分布在91%的主问题中）导致正确解被错误拒评，78%的缺陷需专业物理/数学知识才能识别。作者修正所有可确认缺陷后发布SciCode-Verified，重新评估12个前沿模型，子问题准确率从45-60%升至84-98%，主问题准确率从9-27%升至69-92%，表明此前瓶颈不是模型能力而是评测工具质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SciCode作为科学编码能力的标准评测基准，近年得分已停滞——最强2026模型子问题准确率均聚类在60%左右，后续模型与前任持平。作者将停滞归因于基准本身缺陷而非模型能力限制，通过系统性审计揭示了大规模的错误拒评问题。

Method: 作者对SciCode全部65个测试问题进行了逐问题的领域专家审计，识别出263个缺陷并分类；确认192个导致正确解被误拒（包括金答案不可复现、容差过严、规格自相矛盾等），其中78%需专业物理数学知识才能发现。随后修正所有可确认缺陷生成SciCode-Verified，每项修改记录理由并由第二位领域专家独立复核，最后重新评估12个前沿模型快照。

Result: 修正后基准上，12个前沿模型的子问题准确率从45-60%显著提升至84-98%，主问题准确率从9-27%提升至69-92%。表明前沿模型在科学编码上的实际能力远高于SciCode原有评分所体现的水平。

Conclusion: SciCode原基准的评测质量是导致模型得分停滞的瓶颈，而非模型能力天花板。作者发布附带完整审计记录的SciCode-Verified作为修正后的公共标准，为科学编码评估提供更可靠的评测工具。

Abstract: SciCode is the standard measure of the scientific-coding ability of language models: research-level problems that demand both frontier scientific theory and its implementation as working numerical code. It is a component of the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index and a standing evaluation in government and national-laboratory suites. Yet its scores have recently plateaued: the strongest 2026 models cluster tightly around 60\% subproblem accuracy, and a successor model ties its predecessor. We trace this stagnation to defects in the benchmark itself. A per-problem, domain-expert audit of all 65 test problems uncovers 263 defects; 192 of them, spread across 91\% of the main problems, cause correct, instruction-following solutions to be wrongly rejected---through non-reproducible gold answers, over-tight tolerances, or self-contradictory specifications. Critically, 78\% of these score-suppressing defects require specialized physics or mathematics knowledge to detect, not mere clerical proofreading. We corrected every confirmable defect to produce SciCode-Verified. The corrections add only the specifications a well-posed problem requires, repair grading, and tighten the tests that were too lenient; every change is recorded with its justification and independently re-checked by a second domain expert. We re-evaluate twelve frontier model snapshots on the corrected benchmark and find a substantial recovery: subproblem accuracy rises from 45--60\% to 84--98\%, and main-problem accuracy from 9--27\% to 69--92\%. State-of-the-art models are far more proficient in scientific coding than SciCode has suggested---the bottleneck was not model capability, but the quality of the evaluation instrument. We release SciCode-Verified with its complete audit trail as the corrected public standard.

</details>


### [161] [RepairFormer: Automated Repair of Structured Inputs Using Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05060)
*Ovi Paul,Tom J King,Ali Shokri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepairFormer是一种基于Transformer的框架，用于修复结构化输入文件（如JSON、DOT、OBJ等），通过监督式序列生成、格式标签、oracle验证和边界局部修复技术，在保持内容完整性的同时生成有效输出，修复率88%、恢复率94%，比现有方法快5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化输入文件在软件系统中广泛使用，但这些文件一旦发生轻微损坏，解析器就会拒绝处理，导致测试、分析、部署和下游自动化中断。现有的修复技术虽然能生成结构有效的输入，但往往依赖删除操作或重复搜索，可能丢失原始内容并导致语义错误，因此需要一种既能生成有效输出又能保留原始内容的修复方法。

Method: RepairFormer将输入修复形式化为监督式序列生成任务，采用Transformer架构。核心创新包括：使用格式标签来约束输出结构；利用oracle验证确保生成结果的有效性；采用边界局部修复（boundary-localized repair）工作流，将生成焦点集中在检测到的故障区域，从而减少输入规模并支持修复更长的文件。

Result: 在评估中，RepairFormer在修复任务上达到88%的修复率和94%的恢复率，且在修复成功时展现出最强的内容保留能力。额外的基准测试实验表明，RepairFormer实现97.57%的修复率和94.29%的恢复率，运行速度比现有最佳方法快5倍。

Conclusion: RepairFormer通过将修复任务建模为条件生成问题并引入边界局部修复策略，在修复率、内容恢复率和运行效率上全面超越了现有技术，为结构化输入文件自动修复提供了一种高效且内容保留的解决方案。

Abstract: Structured input files such as JSON, DOT, OBJ, INI, S-expression, and TinyC are widely used in software systems, but small corruptions can cause parsers to reject otherwise useful data. Repairing such inputs is important because malformed configuration, program, and data files can interrupt testing, analysis, deployment, and downstream automation even when most of the original content remains intact. Existing repair techniques can produce structurally valid inputs, but they often rely on deletion or repeated search, which may lose original content and result in semantic incorrectness. This paper presents RepairFormer, a transformer-based framework for structured input repair. The approach formulates repair as a supervised sequence generation task and uses format tags, oracle validation, and boundary-localized repair to generate valid outputs while preserving content. The boundary workflow focuses generation on the detected fault region, reducing the input size, and supporting repair of longer files. In evaluation, RepairFormer achieves a 88% in repair and 94% in recovery, showing strongest content preservation when repairs are successful. Additional experiments on our benchmark shows RepairFormer repairs 97.57% and recovers 94.29% with 5x faster runtime compared to state of the art.

</details>


### [162] [Characterizing Visual Accessibility Issues in AI Developer Tools: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05116)
*Sabrina Haque,Christoph Csallner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析五个AI开发者工具生态系统（GitHub Copilot、Cursor、Claude Code、OpenAI Codex和OpenCode）的2,652个候选问题与论坛讨论，使用三模型集成识别出600个一致确认的视觉无障碍报告，并通过主题建模和定性分析归纳出三类主要障碍类别，揭示了AI开发者工具的可访问性记录受交互设计与生态系统报告维护实践双重影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发者工具日益通过聊天面板、终端代理、生成式diff和流式状态输出等界面进行编程交互，这些界面可能为视障、低视力及色觉缺陷开发者带来视觉可访问性障碍，但目前对这些障碍在公共工具生态系统中如何被报告的情况知之甚少。

Method: 从五个AI开发者工具生态系统中检索2,652个候选问题，采用三模型集成进行筛选，得到600个一致同意的正面视觉可访问性报告，并通过分层人工抽查验证选择策略的保守性；随后使用主题建模和定性分析识别障碍类别，并辅以探索性元数据分析比较各生态系统中报告者活动与维护者参与及关闭流程的差异。

Result: 识别出三类反复出现的可访问性障碍类别：屏幕阅读器与辅助技术障碍；视觉呈现、对比度和区分度问题；AI特定界面中的可读性、缩放和控制限制。这些关注点的相对显著性在不同生态系统中有所差异，反映了编辑器、终端、聊天、diff和代理等交互界面的差异。元数据分析进一步揭示了报告者活动差异以及GitHub生态系统中维护者参与和关闭流程的差异。

Conclusion: AI开发者工具的可访问性记录受到交互设计和周围生态系统的报告与维护实践的共同塑造，研究为理解并改进AI编程工具的可访问性提供了系统性证据。

Abstract: AI-assisted developer tools increasingly mediate programming through chat panels, terminal agents, generated diffs, and streaming status output. These interaction surfaces may create visual accessibility barriers for blind, low-vision, and color-vision-deficient developers, yet little is known about how such barriers are reported in public tool ecosystems. We analyze issues and forum discussions from five AI developer tool ecosystems: GitHub Copilot in VS Code, Cursor, Claude Code, OpenAI Codex, and OpenCode. From 2,652 keyword-retrieved candidates, a three-model ensemble identified 600 unanimously positive visual accessibility reports. A stratified manual sanity check supported this conservative selection. Topic modeling and qualitative analysis identified three recurring categories: screen-reader and assistive-technology barriers; visual presentation, contrast, and differentiation problems; and readability, scaling, and control limitations in AI-specific interfaces. The relative prominence of these concerns varied across ecosystems and reflected differences in editor, terminal, chat, diff, and agent interaction surfaces. An exploratory metadata analysis further identified differences in reporter activity and, across the GitHub-based ecosystems, maintainer participation and closure processes. These findings show that the accessibility record of AI developer tools is shaped by both their interaction design and the reporting and maintenance practices of their surrounding ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Neighborhood-Aware Dual Biomedical Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04144)
*Yicheng Tao,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了PILOT，一个三阶段生物医学实体链接框架，包含邻域感知检索、双重重排序和分数融合，在五个基准数据集上达到平均SOTA且推理高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学实体链接面临三重挑战：知识库实体数量巨大、提及（mention）常存在歧义、不同语料库的标注约定各异。现有方法难以同时应对这些问题，需要一种能高效且准确完成实体链接的框架。

Method: PILOT由三个阶段组成：(1) 邻域感知检索——从查询侧重构提及、从知识库侧池化实体嵌入，向双方注入本体结构信息；(2) 双重重排序——从表面形式和上下文两个互补视角对检索池打分；(3) 分数融合——将两个视角的分数融合得到最终结果。

Result: PILOT在五个广泛使用的基准数据集上的平均性能达到最先进水平（SOTA），同时在推理阶段保持高效。

Conclusion: 通过注入本体结构的三阶段框架，PILOT能够有效应对大规模知识库、提及歧义和语料标注差异等挑战，兼顾准确性与推理效率，为生物医学实体链接提供了新的强基线。

Abstract: Biomedical entity linking grounds mentions in clinical and scientific text to entities in a curated knowledge base (KB) with ontological structure, which supports downstream applications such as literature-scale information extraction and patient-record normalization. The task has several challenges at once: the KB contains large numbers of entities, mentions are often ambiguous, and gold labels follow annotation conventions specific to each corpus. To address these challenges, we propose PILOT, a three-stage framework made up of neighborhood-aware retrieval, dual reranking, and score fusion. The retriever injects ontological structure from both the query and KB side, by reformulating mentions and pooling entity embeddings. The retrieved pool is then scored from two complementary views, one over surface forms and one over context, and fused together. PILOT achieves the state of the art on average across five widely-used benchmarks and remains efficient at inference.

</details>


### [164] [The Price of Isolation: Estimating the Ecosystem Cost of Symmetric Two-Sided A/B Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04432)
*Yuanyuan Shen,Yiren Yan,Wenjie Li,Chunhui Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了双边内容平台中对称双边隔离（将创作者和观众按比例分配到隔离的处理/控制子市场）的参与度成本。作者通过极值理论证明，该成本是否随平台规模增长而消失取决于匹配质量的上尾分布：轻尾或界尾分布下成本随候选池扩大而消失，而重尾分布下成本收敛于一个与规模无关的常数。两个生产实验（A/A流量扫描和单边目录消融）验证了这一结论，并提出了一个实验前预算程序来预估这一成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边隔离是内容平台在创作者端实验和冷启动实验中的常用方法，因为它能消除跨臂市场干扰。但直觉上隔离会减少每个观众可看到的候选目录，这种参与度成本应随平台增长而消失。作者希望严格验证这一直觉是否正确，并量化隔离的实际成本。

Method: 采用极值理论建立订单统计模型来分析匹配质量的尾部行为；通过尾类损失律证明成本消失与否取决于匹配质量的上尾分布类型（轻尾/界尾 vs 重尾）；在拥有数百万活跃创作者的生产平台上进行两个实验验证：纯粹的A/A流量扫描测量深度分级参与度成本，单边目录消融独立验证每观众细化对损失的贡献，并用小探索池标定的尾部指数预测全目录消融的效果。

Result: 极值理论揭示了显著的二分法：轻尾或界尾下损失随候选池增长而消失；重尾下损失收敛于与规模无关的常数，即使候选池扩大几个数量级也无法消除成本。生产实验证据与此一致：A/A扫描显示可测量的深度分级参与度成本；单边目录消融证明每观众细化有独立贡献；用小探索池标定的尾部指数能准确预测更大全目录消融中观察到的效应。

Conclusion: 双边隔离存在实质性的参与度代价，实验者应像对待其他成本一样将其纳入预算。作者提供了预飞行程序：在实验启动前估算隔离成本、据此调整流量规模，并在预测成本超过阈值时推荐备选设计方案。

Abstract: On two-sided content platforms, symmetric two-sided isolation (assigning matched fractions of creators and viewers to isolated treatment and control submarkets) is widely used for creator-side and cold-start experiments because it removes cross-arm marketplace interference. Isolation, however, thins each viewer's candidate catalog, and intuition suggests the resulting engagement cost should fade as the platform grows: a small fraction of a vast catalog is still vast. We show that, in an order-statistics model of engagement, whether this intuition holds depends on the upper tail of match quality. Extreme-value theory yields tail-class loss laws with a sharp dichotomy: for light or bounded tails the loss vanishes as the candidate pool grows, whereas under heavy tails it converges to a size-independent constant, so expanding the candidate pool, even by orders of magnitude, does not asymptotically eliminate the cost. Evidence from two production experiments on a platform with millions of active creators is consistent with this picture: a pure A/A traffic sweep reveals a measurable, depth-graded engagement cost; a one-sided catalog ablation independently shows that per-viewer thinning contributes to the loss; and a tail index calibrated on the small exploration pool predicts an effect consistent with the one observed in the far larger full-catalog ablation. Isolation thus carries a price that experimenters should budget for, like any other cost. We give practitioners a preflight procedure that estimates it before launch, sizes traffic accordingly, and recommends a fallback design when the predicted cost exceeds a chosen tolerance.

</details>


### [165] [Multi-Objective Ranking for Live-Streaming: Balancing Fresh and Delayed Signals with Segment-Aware Targeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04455)
*Xiaoyi Gu,Julia Tavares,Eder Santana,Carlos Mendoza-Cardenas,Nikita Mishra,Saad Ali*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对娱乐直播流媒体推荐系统的多目标优化框架，通过延迟窗口反馈收集、多模型架构与分段感知目标模块、以及MMoE集成三个核心贡献，有效解决了用户行为稀疏、延迟和偏置的问题。在线A/B测试显示DAV提升0.09%，高活跃用户封顶ARPU提升0.56%，并在Twitch移动端直播流上验证了跨平台泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 娱乐直播流媒体的推荐系统面临三大核心挑战：用户行为稀疏且延迟（不同于电商的线性行为序列，直播观众会同时进行观看、聊天、关注和消费等多种并发行为，且延迟各异）；交互数据在不同用户群体间存在偏置；传统单一模型难以同时优化多个相关但目标不同的业务指标。

Method: 论文提出三个关键贡献：1) 延迟窗口方法，将反馈收集扩展到即时响应之外，捕捉延迟行为；2) 多模型架构，融合新鲜信号与延迟信号，并引入分段感知目标模块，针对用户生命周期不同阶段优化排序分数；3) 集成MMoE（多门控混合专家网络），联合建模多个相关目标，同时相比独立模型减少41.9%的模型参数。

Result: 在线A/B测试结果表明：DAV提升0.09%，产生每年数百万额外活跃观众天数；高活跃观众封顶ARPU提升0.56%；观众分段定向为新用户和低活跃观众带来额外0.15%的DAV提升；MMoE增强在整体DAV上增加0.08%，新增关注增加0.27%。在Twitch移动直播流上测试获得+1.12%的正向用户-频道交互（点击、关注、点赞）提升。

Conclusion: 该系统以低延迟处理排名请求，提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，能够在不同用户群体之间平衡多个业务目标。跨平台（Twitch）验证结果表明，该多模型架构具有超越主要应用场景的广泛适用性。

Abstract: One of the most challenging problems entertainment live-streaming services face in recommendation systems is that user behaviors are sparse and delayed, and interaction data exhibits bias for different user segments. Unlike e-commerce applications where user actions follow linear sequences, live-streaming viewers engage in multiple concurrent behaviors of watching, chatting, following, and spending, each occurring with varying delays. We address these challenges through three key contributions: 1) a delayed window approach that extends feedback collection beyond immediate responses, 2) a multi-model architecture that combines fresh and delayed signals, and a segment-aware targeting module that optimizes ranking scores differently across user lifecycle stages, and 3) Multi-gate Mixture-of-Experts (MMoE) integration that jointly models correlated targets while reducing model parameters by 41.9% compared to independent models. Online A/B testing demonstrates significant improvements, including a +0.09% increase in Daily Active Viewers (DAV), generating millions more annual active viewer days, and +0.56% increase in highly engaged viewers' capped Average Revenue Per User (ARPU). Viewer-segment targeting achieved an additional +0.15% DAV improvement for newer and less engaged viewers, while MMoE enhancement added +0.08% overall DAV and +0.27% new follows. The proposed system processes ranking requests with low latency, providing a scalable approach for balancing multiple business objectives across diverse user populations. In addition, we tested the multi-model architecture on the Twitch mobile live feed and achieved a +1.12% increase in positive user-channel interactions (clicks, follows, and likes), demonstrating applicability beyond the primary use case.

</details>


### [166] [Skills Know Their Neighbors: Cluster-Contrastive Capability Pages for Skill Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04482)
*Zifei Wang,Wei Wen,Qiang Ji,Ruizhi Qiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Capability Pages，一种通过重写离线技能库文本来提升大语言模型智能体技能检索与路由能力的方法，无需修改在线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能库不断增长时，大语言模型智能体需要从大量候选技能中检索可复用的技能，但许多候选技能共享相同的主题和词汇却实现不同能力。检索效果不仅受限于评分器，还受限于被评分的文档本身——文档可能只描述了技能能做什么，却未说明哪些相似请求应被路由到其他地方。这种文档层面的限制无法通过改进检索器单独消除。

Method: 将技能的能力形式化为其"可执行区域"（executable region），即该技能能解决的查询集合，并把技能文档视为该区域的有损观测。提出Capability Pages，包含三个组件：正触发器Tpos、负边界Tneg和判别性主体B。这些字段由离线编译器通过比较相邻技能生成。推理时，索引利用Tpos和B进行候选召回，路由器利用Tneg拒绝易混淆的替代技能。

Result: 在SRA-Bench（含26,262个技能和5,400个问题，来自六个数据集）上，Capability Pages在所有五个测试的检索器上均提升了Recall@10，平均提升2.94个点。加入Tneg后，在四个执行器和六个数据集上平均提升端到端任务成功率3.62个点。在中文SSL-SkillDiscovery上的迁移评估中，使用相同编码器在不同条件下达到73.07%的MRR@50。

Conclusion: Capability Pages无需修改在线模型，仅通过重写离线技能库即可改善路由性能，有效减少文档层面的检索误差。

Abstract: As skill libraries grow, large language model agents must retrieve reusable skills from candidates that often share the same topic and vocabulary but implement different capabilities. Retrieval is limited not only by the scorer but also by the text being scored: a document may describe what a skill does without stating which similar requests should be routed elsewhere. We formalize a skill's capability as its \emph{executable region}, the set of queries it can solve, and view its document as a lossy observation of that region. This view exposes a document-imposed component of retrieval error that cannot be removed by improving the retriever alone. We therefore propose \emph{Capability Pages}, cluster-contrastive skill representations containing a positive trigger $\Tpos$, a negative boundary $\Tneg$, and a discriminative body $B$. An offline compiler compares neighboring skills to write these fields. At inference time, the index uses $\Tpos$ and $B$ for candidate recall, while the router uses $\Tneg$ to reject confusable alternatives. On SRA-Bench, which contains 26{,}262 skills and 5{,}400 questions from six datasets, Capability Pages improve Recall@10 for all five tested retrievers, with a mean gain of $2.94$ points. Adding $\Tneg$ to candidate cards improves end-to-end task success by $3.62$ points on average across four executors and six datasets. A transfer evaluation on Chinese SSL-SkillDiscovery reaches $73.07\%$ MRR@50 using the same encoder across conditions. Capability Pages require no modification to the online models; they improve routing by rewriting the offline skill library.

</details>


### [167] [Characterizing the Evolving Landscape of Modern Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04609)
*Shuoqi Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该博士研究旨在系统性地刻画现代信息觅求（IS）过程中的变化，特别是由生成式人工智能（GenAI）驱动的转变，通过在线众包调查实验、理论IS框架及带神经生理信号的在实验室实验等方法，为个性化、认知感知的IS系统设计提供洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GenAI的兴起，现代信息觅求过程发生了显著变化——引入了更多界面、更复杂的交互和扩展的系统能力。作者认为这些变化应当被系统性审视，但目前缺乏对这一演变过程的系统表征研究。

Method: 采用三种互补的研究机制：1）在线众包调查实验，考察搜索界面偏好；2）理论IS框架，建立分析基础；3）带神经生理信号的在实验室实验，测量信息觅求过程中的认知努力。

Result: 研究提供了对当前搜索界面偏好格局的洞见，以及对信息觅求过程中认知投入的理解，特别是GenAI驱动下的变化特征。

Conclusion: 该博士研究将有助于并为未来个性化、认知感知IS系统的设计提供参考与启示。

Abstract: Information seeking (IS) evolves, as does the human IS process. Since the rise of Generative AI (GenAI), modern IS has shifted by introducing more interfaces, more complex interactions, and expanded system capabilities. We argue that these changes in modern IS should be systematically examined. This PhD research characterizes the changes in the modern IS process. We use mechanisms, including online crowdsourcing survey experiments, theoretical IS frameworks, and in-lab experiments with neurophysiological signals, to characterize the shifts in modern IS, especially those driven by GenAI. We offer insights into the current landscape of search interface preferences and the cognitive efforts involved in seeking information. We believe this PhD research will contribute to and inform future designs of personalized, cognition-aware IS systems.

</details>


### [168] [WatchLens: A Configurable Platform for Online Video Recommendation Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04807)
*Deogyong Kim,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: WatchLens 是一个开源平台，解决了视频推荐系统研究中在线实验基础设施的缺口——现有工具无法同时关联播放行为与产生该行为的推荐条件。该平台采用模块化架构，允许用户界面、内容源和推荐策略独立配置，并在录制时通过标准化日志层将推荐策略和排名位置附加到每个事件上，支持对推荐策略如何影响播放行为、会话延续和导航进行会话级分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究视频推荐系统如何塑造用户行为需要进行在线实验，将播放行为与产生该行为的推荐条件联系起来。然而，现有的用户研究基础设施要么只提供行为数据，要么只提供推荐条件，无法在同一实验工作流中同时获得两者，这阻碍了对推荐效果的系统性研究。

Method: WatchLens采用模块化架构，其中用户界面、内容源和推荐策略可独立配置；推荐策略可分别分配给信息流和观看页面；标准化的日志层在记录时即将推荐策略和排名位置附加到每个事件上，使得策略与结果之间的关联在每个事件中直接可用，无需事后重建。平台还支持单服务器部署以保障可复现性。

Result: 通过一个短视频案例研究验证了该平台，该案例保持界面、信息流策略和内容池不变，仅改变观看页面策略，展示了平台如何在会话级别对推荐效果与真实观看行为进行对比分析。

Conclusion: WatchLens作为公开可用的、单服务器可部署的系统，为可复现的在线视频推荐研究提供了基础设施支持，填补了实验工作流中行为记录与推荐条件关联的空白。

Abstract: Studying how video recommender systems shape user behavior requires online experiments that link playback behavior with the recommendation conditions that produced it. Existing user-study infrastructure provides one or the other, but not both within a single experimentation workflow. We present WatchLens, an open-source platform that fills this gap. WatchLens adopts a modular architecture in which user interfaces, content sources, and recommendation policies are independently configurable, with policies assignable separately to the feed and the watch page, while a standardized logging layer attaches the recommendation policy and ranking position to every event at recording time. This design enables researchers to analyze how recommendation policies and ranking positions shape downstream playback behavior, session continuation, and navigation between the feed and the watch page, with the linkage between policy and outcome available in each event rather than reconstructed afterwards. We demonstrate WatchLens with a short-form video case study that holds the interface, feed policy, and content pool constant while varying only the watch-page policy, showing how the platform supports session-level comparison of recommendation effects on real viewing behavior. WatchLens is released as a publicly available, single-server deployable system for reproducible online video recommendation research.

</details>


### [169] [DEGR: Dual Exploration-Driven Generative Re-Ranking for Adaptive Cross-Request Context Bridging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04809)
*Binglei Zhao,Xuanhua Yang,Xiwei Zhao,Sulong Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DEGR（Dual Exploration-Driven Generative Re-Ranking），一种面向工业推荐系统的双探索驱动生成式重排序方法。DEGR通过混合监督-强化探索优化范式，结合探索奖励模型自适应平衡即时价值与探索价值，在低质量供给场景下提升重排序效果，在京东电商系统中实现UCTR提升1.22%、PV提升0.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排序方法受限于固定上游供给，在低质量供给条件下难以进一步提升效果，缺乏主动平衡即时价值与探索价值的机制，导致浏览潜力未被充分利用且错失偶然转化机会。

Method: DEGR采用混合监督-强化探索优化范式，由探索奖励模型引导自适应平衡即时与探索价值。核心包含三个组件：监督学习、探索多样性约束、以及自适应奖励加权ORPO偏好优化。通过双重探索机制，生成器充当跨请求的上下文桥梁。

Result: 离线和在线实验表明DEGR优于SOTA方法，在京东电商推荐系统中UCTR提升最高1.22%，PV提升0.20%。

Conclusion: 通过主动探索低质量供给条件下的曝光机会，DEGR能够更有效保留浏览潜力并促进意外转化，验证了探索驱动的重排序范式在工业推荐系统中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: In industrial recommendation systems, the re-ranking stage balances business objectives and diversity for sequence-level optimization while modeling contextual information. However, constrained by fixed upstream supply, existing methods fail to deliver further effectiveness gains, especially under low-quality supply. To overcome this, re-ranking can actively balance immediate and exploratory value, for instance, by prioritizing exploratory exposure under low-quality supply to preserve browsing potential and facilitate serendipitous conversions. Therefore, we propose a Dual Exploration-Driven Generative Re-Ranking (DEGR) method. DEGR adopts a hybrid supervised-reinforcement exploration and optimization paradigm, guided by an exploratory reward model that adaptively balances immediate and exploratory value. The hybrid optimization paradigm integrates three key components: supervised learning, exploration diversity constraint, and adaptive reward-weighted ORPO for preference optimization. Through this dual exploration, the generator ultimately acts as an adaptive cross-request contextual bridge. Offline and online experiments indicate that DEGR outperforms SOTA methods, achieving improvements of up to 1.22% UCTR and 0.20% PV in the JD E-commerce recommendation system.

</details>


### [170] [MemoryCPT: An End-to-End Agent Memory Framework for Cost-Performance Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04843)
*Songxin Lei,Kun Ouyang,Weilin Ruan,Yuqian Wu,Zhijiang Guo,Yushi Sun,Fugee Tsung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoryCPT，一个端到端可训练的长视界LLM智能体记忆管线，通过两阶段设计（查询无关蒸馏QAD和查询感知检索与摘要QAR）改进记忆恢复的效率与性能，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval基准上显著优化了成本-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界LLM智能体需要从大规模交互历史中恢复有用证据，同时避免向下游模型传递过多上下文。现有记忆管线依赖手工启发式规则和重复LLM调用，导致冗余上下文和高推理成本，缺乏端到端可训练的解决方案。

Method: MemoryCPT包含两个阶段：(1) 查询无关蒸馏（QAD），利用显式推理痕迹将一个模块化记忆构建管线蒸馏为紧凑模型；(2) 查询感知检索与摘要（QAR），结合倒数排名融合（RRF）和基于LoRA的摘要器，采用分组相对策略优化（GRPO）在成本感知奖励下训练。此外引入质量每成本（QPC）指标量化单位推理成本下的答案质量。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval基准上的实验表明，MemoryCPT在成本-性能权衡上优于评估的基线方法，消融和敏感性分析进一步表征了各组件贡献及关键设计选择的影响。

Conclusion: MemoryCPT证明了端到端训练的记忆管线能够有效改善长视界LLM智能体记忆系统的成本-性能平衡，为记忆构建与检索的联合优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require memory systems that recover useful evidence from large interaction histories without passing excessive context to downstream models. Existing memory pipelines often rely on hand-crafted heuristics and repeated LLM calls, which can introduce redundant context and high inference cost. We propose MemoryCPT, an end-to-end trainable agent memory pipeline that spans offline memory construction and online query-conditioned context generation. MemoryCPT consists of two stages: Query-agnostic Distillation (QAD), which distills a modular memory-construction pipeline into a compact model using explicit reasoning traces; and Query-aware Retrieval and Summarization (QAR), which combines reciprocal rank fusion (RRF) with a LoRA-based summarizer trained via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) under a cost-aware reward. We further introduce Quality per Cost (QPC) to quantify answer quality per unit inference cost. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show that MemoryCPT improves the cost-performance trade-off over the evaluated baselines, while ablation and sensitivity analyses characterize the contributions of its components and the effects of key design choices.

</details>
